Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in bayesian_statistics theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 209 and the invariant I(A,B) = 619 mod 3.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 209 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the bayesian_statistics structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 619. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 3. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
